207	      recipe.create = map[m[1]] ?? +m[1];
208	    } else if ((m = l.match(/^currentRecipe\.SetIngredients\((primaryMaterial|\w+), (\d+)\);$/))) {
209	      recipe.items.push({ id: map[m[1]] ?? +m[1], stack: +m[2] });
210	    } else if ((m = l.match(/^currentRecipe\.SetIngredients\((obj\d*)\);$/))) {
211	      const arr = arrVals[m[1]] ?? [];
212	      for (let k = 0; k + 1 < arr.length; k += 2) recipe.items.push({ id: arr[k], stack: arr[k + 1] });
213	    } else if ((m = l.match(/^currentRecipe\.SetCraftingStation\(\(overrideStation >= 0\) \? overrideStation : (\d+)\);$/))) {
214	      recipe.tile = map.overrideStation >= 0 ? map.overrideStation : +m[1];
215	    } else if ((m = l.match(/^currentRecipe\.SetCraftingStation\((\d+)\);$/))) {
216	      recipe.tile = +m[1];
217	    } else if ((m = l.match(/^currentRecipe\.RequireGroup\(RecipeGroups\.(\w+)\);$/))) {
218	      recipe.groups.push(GROUP_ID[m[1]]);
219	    } else if ((m = l.match(/^currentRecipe\.createItem\.stack\s*=\s*(\d+);$/))) {
220	      recipe.createStack = +m[1];
221	    }
222	  }
223	}
224	
225	// ---- AddCritterStatueRecipe（:16571-16587：50 石头+5 小动物(或组) @重型工作台283+墓地）----
226	// 调用形 AddCritterStatueRecipe(statueItem, critterItem=0[, RecipeGroups.X])——
227	// 组形第三个参取组占位物（GetPlaceholderItemType = 组内首个 id）并 RequireGroup
228	function expandCritterStatue(line) {
229	  const m = line.match(/AddCritterStatueRecipe\((\d+),\s*(\d+)(?:,\s*RecipeGroups\.(\w+))?\)/);
230	  if (!m) return;
231	  const statue = +m[1];
232	  let critter = +m[2];
233	  const groups = [];
234	  if (m[3]) {
235	    const gname = m[3];
236	    const gid2 = GROUP_ID[gname];
237	    if (gid2 === undefined) return;
238	    critter = (GROUP_ITEMS[gid2] ?? [0])[0]; // GetPlaceholderItemType = 组内首个
239	    groups.push(gid2);
240	  }
241	  if (critter <= 0 && !groups.length) return;
242	  out.push({
243	    create: statue, createStack: 1, tile: 283,
244	    items: [{ id: 3, stack: 50 }, { id: critter || 1000000 + (groups[0] ?? 0), stack: 5 }],
245	    groups, graveyard: true,
246	  });
247	}
248	
249	const main = methodBody('public static void SetupRecipes()');
250	parseLinear(main);
251	// main 里的直调 AddStandardFurnitureSetRecipes(...) 展开（缺省参补 -1）
252	{
253	  const re = /AddStandardFurnitureSetRecipes\(([^)]*)\);/g;
254	  let m;
255	  while ((m = re.exec(main))) {
256	    const args = m[1].split(',').map((x) => x.trim());
257	    while (args.length < 22) args.push('-1');
258	    expandStandardSet(args);
259	  }
260	}
261	
262	// ---- 反向配方（CreateReversePlatformRecipes :16589 / CreateReverseWallRecipes :16613）----
263	// 条件：单材料配方（items[1] 不存在）且 成品是平台（createTile∈Platforms）或墙（createWall>0）
264	//      且材料本身不是墙（墙反做时不重复反转）。产物 = 材料作成品、原成品作材料、站点同原配方。
265	// createTile/createWall 需 ItemID → Item.cs SetDefaults 的 createTile/createWall 字段：
266	// 从 itemcombat json 无此字段——直接从 Item.cs 提取（正则取 `createTile = N;`/`createWall = N;`）
267	
268	// Item.cs createTile/createWall 扫描（SetDefaults1..5 分段 switch 内）
269	const itemSrc = readFileSync(`${ROOT}/Terarria1456/Terraria/Item.cs`, 'utf8');
270	const CREATE_TILE = {};
271	const CREATE_WALL = {};
272	{
273	  // 分段方法体逐 case 提取：case N: ... createTile = M;
274	  const caseRe = /case (\d+):([\s\S]*?)(?=case \d+:|default:|\n\t\tbreak;)/g;
275	  let m2;
276	  while ((m2 = caseRe.exec(itemSrc))) {
277	    const id = +m2[1];
278	    const body = m2[2].slice(0, 800);
279	    const t = body.match(/createTile\s*=\s*(\d+);/);
280	    if (t) CREATE_TILE[id] = +t[1];
281	    const w = body.match(/createWall\s*=\s*(\d+);/);
282	    if (w) CREATE_WALL[id] = +w[1];
283	  }
284	}
285	// 平台 tile 集（TileID.Sets.Platforms——Main 静态初始化；用 TEdit tiles.json isPlatform 无，
286	// 近似：墙/平台由 Item.cs case 中 createTile 指向的 tile 属于平台族。权威源：Item.cs
287	// 中 `createTile = 19`（木平台）外的平台由 tile 表判断——此处用 TileID.Sets 初始化源：
288	// Terraria.ID/TileID.Sets.Platforms 在 1456 由 Main.cs 静态块填充）
289	const PLATFORM_TILES = (() => {
290	  const s = new Set();
291	  // Main.cs 静态 Platforms[19]=true 等初始化
292	  const mainSrc = readFileSync(`${ROOT}/Terarria1456/Terraria/Main.cs`, 'utf8');
293	  for (const m3 of mainSrc.matchAll(/Platforms\[(\d+)\]\s*=\s*true;/g)) s.add(+m3[1]);
294	  return s;
295	})();
296	
297	const reverseOut = [];
298	const baseCount = out.length;
299	for (let i = 0; i < baseCount; i++) {
300	  const r = out[i];
301	  if (r.items.length !== 1) continue;
302	  const mat = r.items[0];
303	  const isPlatform = CREATE_TILE[r.create] !== undefined && PLATFORM_TILES.has(CREATE_TILE[r.create]);
304	  const isWall = (CREATE_WALL[r.create] ?? 0) > 0 && (CREATE_WALL[mat.id] ?? -1) === -1;
305	  if (!isPlatform && !isWall) continue;
306	  reverseOut.push({
307	    create: mat.id,
308	    createStack: mat.stack,
309	    tile: r.tile,
310	    items: [{ id: r.create, stack: r.createStack }],
311	    groups: [],
312	    // 原版反向配方注册后立即置 notDecraftable（Recipe.cs:16608/16632）——
313	    // 平台/墙反转配方不进 IsCrafted，微光 decraft 不反它们
314	    notDecraftable: true,
315	  });
316	}
317	out.push(...reverseOut);
318	
319	// ---- 输出端去重（同 create+tile+材料序列 视为同一条——原版源码本身有重复注册，如
320	//      4022 Seafood Dinner@烹饪锅 重复 14 次；运行时 list 重复渲染无意义）----
321	{
322	  const seen = new Set();
323	  const unique = [];
324	  for (const r of out) {
325	    const key = `${r.create}|${r.tile}|${r.items.map((i) => `${i.id}x${i.stack}`).sort().join(',')}`;
326	    if (seen.has(key)) continue;
327	    seen.add(key);
328	    unique.push(r);
329	  }
330	  out.length = 0;
331	  out.push(...unique);
332	}
333	
334	const doc = {
335	  count: out.length,
336	  groups: GROUP_ID,
337	  groupItems: GROUP_ITEMS,
338	  tileCountsAs: TILE_COUNTS_AS,
339	  recipes: out,
340	};
341	writeFileSync(`${ROOT}/game/src/data/vanilla-recipes.json`, JSON.stringify(doc));
342	console.error(`配方 ${out.length} 条 / 组 ${Object.keys(GROUP_ID).length} / 站点继承 ${Object.keys(TILE_COUNTS_AS).length}`);
343	console.error('样例:', JSON.stringify(out[0]), JSON.stringify(out[1] ?? null));
344	

```

function flattenLoops(lines) {
  const out = [];
  let i = 0;
  while (i < lines.length) {
    const l = lines[i].trim();
    const m = l.match(/^for \(int (\w+) = (-?\d+); \1 (<=|<|>=|>) (-?\d+); \1\+\+\)$/);
    if (m) {
      // collect body until brace balance
      const body = [];
      let depth = 0, started = false, j = i;
      for (; j < lines.length; j++) {
        const raw = lines[j];
        for (const ch of raw) { if (ch === '{') { depth++; started = true; } else if (ch === '}') depth--; }
        if (started && depth === 0) break;
      }
      body.push(...lines.slice(i + 1, j));
      ...iterate values, push substituted lines...
      i = j + 1;
      continue;
    }
    out.push(lines[i]); i++;
  }
  return out;
}

20	  /** decraft 族(2026-08-13 提取器补提,Recipe.cs 裸赋值 :1064/:1140/:1145) */
21	  notDecraftable?: boolean;
22	  crimson?: boolean;
23	  corruption?: boolean;
24	  /** AddCustomShimmerResult(:173)——decraft 产物覆盖表 [[id,stack],...] */
25	  shimmer?: Array<[number, number]>;
26	}
27	
28	const DOC = recipesJson as unknown as {
29	  count: number;
30	  groups: Record<string, number>;
31	  groupItems: Record<string, number[]>;
32	  tileCountsAs: Record<string, number>;
33	  recipes: VanillaRecipe[];
34	};
35	export const VANILLA_RECIPES = DOC.recipes;
36	export const RECIPE_GROUPS = DOC.groups;
37	export const RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS = DOC.groupItems;
38	export const TILE_COUNTS_AS = DOC.tileCountsAs;
39	
40	/** 站点继承递归展开（Player.SetAdjTile :35192-35208）：tile 本身 + CountsAs 链全部置位 */
41	// ================= decraft 索引表（Recipe.UpdateWhichItemsAreCrafted :15110-15127 1:1） =================
42	// 注册序遍历后写 = last-wins（原版同款）; notDecraftable 跳过 IsCrafted;
43	// crimson/corruption 配方写各自专属表（GetDecraftingRecipeIndex 按世界 evil 取用）。
44	// 惰性构建一次（模块加载即建,3173 条线性扫）。
45	const IS_CRAFTED = new Map<number, number>();
46	const IS_CRAFTED_CRIMSON = new Map<number, number>();
47	const IS_CRAFTED_CORRUPTION = new Map<number, number>();
48	{
49	  const rs = DOC.recipes;
50	  for (let i = 0; i < rs.length; i++) {
51	    const r = rs[i];
52	    if (!r.notDecraftable) IS_CRAFTED.set(r.create, i);
53	    if (r.crimson) IS_CRAFTED_CRIMSON.set(r.create, i);
54	    if (r.corruption) IS_CRAFTED_CORRUPTION.set(r.create, i);
55	  }
56	}
57	
58	/** ShimmerTransforms.GetDecraftingRecipeIndex（ShimmerTransforms.cs:15-31）:
59	 *  IsCrafted<0 → -1;猩红世界取 crimson 表(≥0 才用),腐化世界同理,否则 IsCrafted */
60	export function getDecraftingRecipeIndex(vid: number, crimsonWorld: boolean): number {
61	  const base = IS_CRAFTED.get(vid) ?? -1;
62	  if (base < 0) return -1;
63	  if (crimsonWorld) {
64	    const c = IS_CRAFTED_CRIMSON.get(vid) ?? -1;
65	    if (c >= 0) return c;
66	  } else {
67	    const c = IS_CRAFTED_CORRUPTION.get(vid) ?? -1;
68	    if (c >= 0) return c;
69	  }
70	  return base;
71	}
72	
73	/** 按索引取配方（decraft 执行端用） */
74	export function recipeAt(index: number): VanillaRecipe | undefined {
75	  return DOC.recipes[index];
76	}
77	
78	export function expandStationTiles(tile: number): number[] {
79	  const out = [tile];
80	  let cur = tile;
81	  for (let hop = 0; hop < 6; hop++) {
82	    const next = TILE_COUNTS_AS[String(cur)];
83	    if (next === undefined) break;
84	    out.push(next);
85	    cur = next;
86	  }
87	  return out;
88	}
89	
90	/** vi_<id> key → 原版 item id 反解 */
91	export function vanillaIdOfItemKey(key: string): number {
92	  const m = key.match(/^vi_(\d+)_/);
93	  return m ? Number(m[1]) : -1;
94	}
95	
96	// ================= 附近箱子合成联动（CraftFromNearbyChests，1.4.5.6 默认开） =================
97	// Player.Settings.CraftFromNearbyChests（Player.cs:359 默认 true）→ Recipe.CollectItemsFromChests
98	// （Recipe.cs:378-405）把附近箱/bank 聚进 Recipe._ownedItems；扣料走 CraftingRequests.Consume
99	// （CraftingRequests.cs:220-235，先背包后箱）。ChestData 结构上即满足本接口（items 槽数组）。
100	
101	/** 单个合成材料源（Recipe._recipeChests 的一项）：世界箱 ChestData 或玩家 bank 槽数组 */
102	export interface CraftItemSource {
103	  /** 材料 slot 数组（Chest.item / bank；消费阶段原位扣减——stack 部分扣 / 整格清空） */
104	  items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null>;
105	}
106	
107	/** 合成范围常量（NearbyChests.cs:14/:37 range 缺省 600f）——单位 px（16px/格 → 37.5 格） */
108	export const CRAFT_CHEST_RANGE_PX = 600;
109	
110	/**
111	 * NearbyChests.GetChestsInRangeOf（NearbyChests.cs:10-31）1:1：
112	 * 箱锚 = (x*16+16, y*16+16)（注意是锚格**右缘**，非格中心 x*16+8），与玩家
113	 * position 的欧氏距离 > range 即排除。IsLockedOrInUse（Chest.cs:171-183）：
114	 * locked → 排除；被任一玩家开着 → 排除（单人 = 当前开箱，由 openChest 首位收录，
115	 * 此处按引用去重一并覆盖，等价 Recipe.cs:395 的 Contains 去重 + IsPlayerInChest 门）。
116	 */
117	export function nearbyChestsForCrafting(
118	  chests: readonly ChestData[], px: number, py: number,
119	  rangePx = CRAFT_CHEST_RANGE_PX, openChest?: CraftItemSource | null,
120	): ChestData[] {
121	  const out: ChestData[] = [];
122	  const r2 = rangePx * rangePx;
123	  for (const c of chests) {
124	    if (c.locked) continue;
125	    if (openChest && c === openChest) continue;
126	    const dx = c.x * 16 + 16 - px, dy = c.y * 16 + 16 - py;
127	    if (dx * dx + dy * dy > r2) continue;   // Vector2.Distance > range（平方比较免开方）
128	    out.push(c);
129	  }
130	  return out;
131	}
132	
133	/** 玩家储物 tile → bank 槽位（NearbyChests.cs:68-82：29 存钱罐/97 保险箱/
134	 *  463 守护者熔炉/491 虚空vault；容器索引约定 -2..-5 → bank..bank4） */
135	const BANK_TILE_SHEETS: ReadonlyArray<readonly [number, number]> = [[29, 0], [97, 1], [463, 2], [491, 3]];
136	/** GetBanksInRangeOf tile 段（NearbyChests.cs:41-43）：num = (int)(range/16+2) = 39（600px），
137	 *  玩家中心格 ±39 的 79×79 盒；WorldGen.InWorld 越界跳过 */
138	function bankSourcesForCrafting(
139	  store: Pick<TileStore, 'w' | 'h' | 'inBounds' | 'idx' | 'type'>,
140	  centerTileX: number, centerTileY: number,
141	  banks: ReadonlyArray<CraftItemSource | null | undefined> | undefined,
142	  rangePx: number, voidVaultHeld: boolean,
143	): CraftItemSource[] {
144	  if (!banks) return [];
145	  const num = Math.trunc(rangePx / 16 + 2);
146	  const out: CraftItemSource[] = [];
147	  // 内部 tile id 预解析（sheet 29/97/463/491；未注册 → 该 bank 永不命中）
148	  const internal: number[] = BANK_TILE_SHEETS.map(([sheet]) => SHEET_TO_INTERNAL.get(sheet) ?? -1);
149	  for (let j = centerTileX - num; j < centerTileX - num + num * 2 + 1; j++) {
150	    for (let k = centerTileY - num; k < centerTileY - num + num * 2 + 1; k++) {
151	      if (!store.inBounds(j, k)) continue;
152	      const t = store.type[store.idx(j, k)];
153	      for (let bi = 0; bi < BANK_TILE_SHEETS.length; bi++) {
154	        if (t !== internal[bi]) continue;
155	        // ContainerIndexToPlayerBank（NearbyChests.cs:105-134）：bank 缺席跳过；
156	        // case -5（void vault）：背包持有 vault 物品 5325 → 该 bank 不计（:119-130）
157	        const bank = banks[BANK_TILE_SHEETS[bi][1]];
158	        if (!bank) break;
159	        if (BANK_TILE_SHEETS[bi][1] === 3 && voidVaultHeld) break;
160	        if (!out.includes(bank)) out.push(bank);
161	        break;
162	      }
163	    }
164	  }
165	  return out;
166	}
167	
168	export interface CraftSourcesOpts {
169	  /** 当前打开容器（Recipe.cs:381-384：player.chest != -1 → GetCurrentContainer
170	   *  Player.cs:33036-33047 首入列；Game 开 bank 面板时传 {x:-2-bi, items:banks[bi]} 同源） */
171	  openChest?: CraftItemSource | null;
172	  /** 玩家 bank×4（piggy/safe/forge/void vault；null/缺省 = 无该 bank） */
173	  banks?: ReadonlyArray<CraftItemSource | null | undefined>;
174	  /** 背包持有虚空袋 4131（useVoidBag Player.cs:22892-22902）→ bank4 计入（Recipe.cs:385-388，
175	   *  与当前开的是否 bank4 无关的语义由 openIsBank4 表达） */
176	  voidBagHeld?: boolean;
177	  /** 当前打开容器就是 bank4（player.chest == -5）→ 虚空袋规则不再重复计入（:386 门） */
178	  openIsBank4?: boolean;
179	  /** 背包持有虚空vault物品 5325 → 放置的 vault 槽不计（ContainerIndexToPlayerBank :119-130） */
180	  voidVaultHeld?: boolean;
181	  /** bank tile 扫描源（GetBanksInRangeOf tile 段 :61-89）；缺省 = 跳过 bank 扫描 */
182	  store?: Pick<TileStore, 'w' | 'h' | 'inBounds' | 'idx' | 'type'>;
183	  /** 玩家中心 tile（GetBanksInRangeOf :42 player.Center.ToTileCoordinates()） */
184	  centerTileX?: number;
185	  centerTileY?: number;
186	}
187	
188	/**
189	 * Recipe._recipeChests 组装序（Recipe.cs:378-405 CollectItemsFromChests 1:1）：
190	 *  ① 当前打开容器 → ② 虚空袋持有且未开 bank4 → bank4 → ③ 附近 bank tile（盒判定）
191	 *  → ④ 附近世界箱（600px 圈，locked/占用排除）。px/py = 玩家 position（左上角 px）。
192	 */
193	export function craftSourcesFor(
194	  chests: readonly ChestData[], px: number, py: number, opts: CraftSourcesOpts = {},
195	): CraftItemSource[] {
196	  const out: CraftItemSource[] = [];
197	  if (opts.openChest) out.push(opts.openChest);
198	  if (opts.voidBagHeld && !opts.openIsBank4 && opts.banks?.[3]) out.push(opts.banks[3]);
199	  if (opts.store && opts.centerTileX !== undefined && opts.centerTileY !== undefined) {
200	    for (const b of bankSourcesForCrafting(opts.store, opts.centerTileX, opts.centerTileY,
201	      opts.banks, CRAFT_CHEST_RANGE_PX, !!opts.voidVaultHeld)) {
202	      if (!out.includes(b)) out.push(b);
203	    }
204	  }
205	  for (const c of nearbyChestsForCrafting(chests, px, py, CRAFT_CHEST_RANGE_PX, opts.openChest)) {
206	    out.push(c);
207	  }
208	  return out;
209	}
210	
211	/** 玩家持有的原版 id 计数表（含 RecipeGroup 假 id 聚合，Recipe.cs:408-415）。
212	 *  sources = 附近合成材料源（Recipe._recipeChests，Recipe.cs:377-405 CollectItemsFromChests
213	 *  → :418-431 CollectItems 箱槽在背包之后聚合；再跑假 id 聚合 :409-415） */
214	export function collectOwnedItems(
215	  inv: Inventory, sources?: readonly CraftItemSource[],
216	): Map<number, number> {
217	  const owned = new Map<number, number>();
218	  const add = (vid: number, n: number) => owned.set(vid, (owned.get(vid) ?? 0) + n);
219	  const inc = (key: string, stack: number) => {
220	    const def = ITEM_DEFS[inv[key as never] as unknown as number];
221	    void def;
222	  };
223	  void inc;
224	  // slots 0-57（背包+钱币+弹药）
225	  for (const s of inv.slots) {
226	    if (!s) continue;
227	    const def = ITEM_DEFS[s.id];
228	    if (!def) continue;
229	    const vid = def.vid ?? (def.key.startsWith('vi_') ? parseInt(def.key.slice(3), 10) : -1);
230	    if (vid >= 0) add(vid, s.stack);
231	  }
232	  // 箱/bank 槽（CollectItems(recipeChest.item, recipeChest.maxItems)——stack>0 才计）
233	  if (sources) {
234	    for (const src of sources) {
235	      for (const it of src.items) {
236	        if (!it || it.stack <= 0) continue;
237	        const def = ITEM_DEFS[it.id];
238	        if (!def) continue;
239	        const vid = def.vid ?? (def.key.startsWith('vi_') ? parseInt(def.key.slice(3), 10) : -1);
240	        if (vid >= 0) add(vid, it.stack);
241	      }
242	    }
243	  }
244	  // RecipeGroup 假 id 聚合（组内所有物品持有数之和；须在箱聚合之后重算）
245	  for (const [gidS, items] of Object.entries(RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS)) {
246	    let sum = 0;
247	    for (const it of items) sum += owned.get(it) ?? 0;
248	    if (sum > 0) add(1000000 + Number(gidS), sum);
249	  }
250	  return owned;
251	}
252	
253	/** 材料槽 → 所属组（CreateRequiredItemQuickLookups :15043-15056：槽 id 命中本配方
254	 *  接受组的 ValidItems → 该槽重标记为组）。哨兵 id(≥1000000,提取器遗留)按同义处理 */
255	function groupOf(r: VanillaRecipe, reqId: number): number {
256	  if (reqId >= 1000000) return reqId - 1000000;
257	  for (const g of r.groups) {
258	    if ((RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(g)] ?? []).includes(reqId)) return g;
259	  }
260	  return -1;
261	}
262	
263	/** 材料匹配（RequiredItemEntry.Matches Recipe.cs:35-42）：组槽 = 组内全体持有量之和
264	 *  （collectOwnedItems 已按假 id 聚合）——持 Boreal 木(5215)可满足 Wood 组槽 */
265	function itemMatches(r: VanillaRecipe, reqId: number, owned: Map<number, number>): number {
266	  const g = groupOf(r, reqId);
267	  if (g >= 0) return owned.get(1000000 + g) ?? 0;
268	  return owned.get(reqId) ?? 0;
269	}
270	
271	/** 单条配方当前可合成次数（HowManyTimesCanRecipeBeCrafted Recipe.cs:289-310）；
272	 *  返回 0 = 材料不足 */
273	export function craftableCount(r: VanillaRecipe, owned: Map<number, number>): number {
274	  let min = Infinity;
275	  for (const req of r.items) {
276	    const have = itemMatches(r, req.id, owned);
277	    if (have < req.stack) return 0;
278	    min = Math.min(min, Math.floor(have / req.stack));
279	  }
280	  return min === Infinity ? 0 : min;
281	}
282	
283	/** 站点 TileID → 本仓库内部 tile id（TILE_BY_KEY 反查 v_<id>_ key 不稳定，按 vanilla.sheet 扫 TILE_DEFS） */
284	const SHEET_TO_INTERNAL = (() => {
285	  const m = new Map<number, number>();
286	  for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {
287	    const sheet = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;
288	    if (sheet !== undefined && !m.has(sheet)) m.set(sheet, i);
289	  }
290	  return m;
291	})();
292	
293	export interface VanillaCraftCtx {
294	  /** 附近站点（内部 tile id 集合——Game.stationsNearby 扫描结果） */
295	  nearbyInternal: Set<number>;
296	  /** 附近有炼金台（tile 355/699 经继承算 tile 13；player.alchemyTable）——炼金配方材料 1/3 免费 */
297	  alchemyTable?: boolean;
298	  /** 附近液体：水/蜂蜜/岩浆源（adjWaterSource/adjHoney/adjLava） */
299	  nearWater?: boolean;
300	  nearHoney?: boolean;
301	  nearLava?: boolean;
302	  zoneSnow?: boolean;
303	  zoneGraveyard?: boolean;
304	}
305	
306	/** 环境条件（PlayerMeetsEnvironmentConditions Recipe.cs:321-357） */
307	export function envOk(r: VanillaRecipe, ctx: VanillaCraftCtx): boolean {
308	  if (r.tile >= 0) {
309	    const expanded = expandStationTiles(r.tile);
310	    // 任一等价站点在附近（adjTile[requiredTile] + CountsAs 继承）
311	    const ok = expanded.some((t) => {
312	      const internal = SHEET_TO_INTERNAL.get(t);
313	      return internal !== undefined && ctx.nearbyInternal.has(internal);
314	    });
315	    if (!ok) return false;
316	  }
317	  if (r.water && !ctx.nearWater) return false;
318	  if (r.honey && !ctx.nearHoney) return false;
319	  if (r.lava && !ctx.nearLava) return false;
320	  if (r.snow && !ctx.zoneSnow) return false;
321	  if (r.graveyard && !ctx.zoneGraveyard) return false;
322	  return true;
323	}
324	
325	/** 成品 → 本仓库 item key（vi_<id>_* 反查） */
326	const VID_TO_KEY = (() => {
327	  const m = new Map<number, string>();
328	  for (const def of ITEM_DEFS) {
329	    const vid = def.vid ?? (def.key.startsWith('vi_') ? parseInt(def.key.slice(3), 10) : -1);
330	    if (vid >= 0 && !m.has(vid)) m.set(vid, def.key);
331	  }
332	  return m;
333	})();
334	
335	export function vanillaItemKey(vid: number): string | undefined {
336	  return VID_TO_KEY.get(vid);
337	}
338	
339	/** 材料原版 id → 显示名用的本仓库 key（组内物品可能部分缺，取第一个存在的） */
340	export function ingredientKey(vid: number): string | undefined {
341	  if (vid >= 1000000) {
342	    const items = RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(vid - 1000000)];
343	    if (items) for (const it of items) {
344	      const k = VID_TO_KEY.get(it);
345	      if (k) return k;
346	    }
347	    return undefined;
348	  }
349	  return VID_TO_KEY.get(vid);
350	}
351	
352	/** 计算可用配方索引列表（UpdateRecipeList 主干）。
353	 *  sources = 附近箱/bank（CraftFromNearbyChests，Player.cs:359 默认开）——
354	 *  箱材料计入可用性（CollectItemsToCraftWithFrom Recipe.cs:373-377） */
355	export function availableRecipes(
356	  inv: Inventory, ctx: VanillaCraftCtx, sources?: readonly CraftItemSource[],
357	): Array<{ r: VanillaRecipe; count: number }> {
358	  const owned = collectOwnedItems(inv, sources);
359	  const out: Array<{ r: VanillaRecipe; count: number }> = [];
360	  for (const r of VANILLA_RECIPES) {
361	    if (!envOk(r, ctx)) continue;
362	    const count = craftableCount(r, owned);
363	    if (count > 0) out.push({ r, count });
364	  }
365	  return out;
366	}
367	
368	/** 单材料匹配（RequiredItemEntry.Matches Recipe.cs:35-42 的实例判定侧）：
369	 *  组槽 = 组内任一成员物品；单槽 = 精确 vanilla id。itemId = 本仓库内部 item id */
370	function reqMatchesItem(r: VanillaRecipe, reqId: number, itemId: number): boolean {
371	  const g = groupOf(r, reqId);
372	  if (g < 0) return invIndexOfVanilla(reqId) === itemId;
373	  const members = RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(g)];
374	  if (!members) return false;
375	  for (const m of members) if (invIndexOfVanilla(m) === itemId) return true;
376	  return false;
377	}
378	
379	/** 跨源计数（CraftingRequests.CountMatches :206-218 + CollectItems 双源聚合）：
380	 *  背包 0-57 槽 + 各材料源槽，按 req 匹配求和 */
381	function countReqAcross(
382	  r: VanillaRecipe, reqId: number, inv: Inventory, sources?: readonly CraftItemSource[],
383	): number {
384	  let n = 0;
385	  const g = groupOf(r, reqId);
386	  if (g >= 0) {
387	    for (const m of (RECIPE_GROUP_ITEMS[String(g)] ?? [])) {
388	      const iid = invIndexOfVanilla(m);
389	      if (iid >= 0) n += inv.countOf(iid);
390	    }
391	  } else {
392	    const iid = invIndexOfVanilla(reqId);
393	    if (iid >= 0) n += inv.countOf(iid);
394	  }
395	  if (sources) {
396	    for (const src of sources) {
397	      for (const it of src.items) {
398	        if (!it || it.stack <= 0) continue;
399	        if (reqMatchesItem(r, reqId, it.id)) n += it.stack;
400	      }
401	    }
402	  }
403	  return n;
404	}
405	
406	/** 执行合成（CraftingRequests.CraftLocally 主干）：扣材料→返成品 key（放置进背包由调用方）。
407	 *  sources = 附近箱/bank（CraftFromNearbyChests）：校验计入箱材料，扣料先背包后箱
408	 *  （CraftingRequests.Consume :220-235），箱内按槽序部分扣/整格清空（:242-285） */
409	export function consumeMaterials(
410	  inv: Inventory, r: VanillaRecipe, times = 1, alchemyTable = false,
411	  sources?: readonly CraftItemSource[],
412	): boolean {
413	  // ---- 第一阶段：可用性校验（不扣）——CraftingRequests.cs:86-102 每次迭代重查、
414	  // 永不过扣。旧实现"边扣边查、不足 return false 不回滚"=材料部分销毁零产出
415	  // （shift ×10 合成时整批蒸发,2026-08-13 修）----
416	  for (const req of r.items) {
417	    const need = req.stack * times;
418	    if (countReqAcross(r, req.id, inv, sources) < need) return false;
419	  }
420	  // ---- 第二阶段：执行扣除（校验已过,理论不触不足;保持原折扣/组扣语义）----
421	  for (const req of r.items) {
422	    let need = req.stack * times;
423	    // 炼金折扣（GetIngredientCraftingDiscount Recipe.cs:213-227：alchemy 配方且 alchemyTable
424	    // 时每个材料独立掷 1/3 概率该份免费——按"每份独立掷骰"逐个扣）
425	    if (r.alchemy && alchemyTable && times === 1) {
426	      let discounted = 0;
427	      for (let k = 0; k < need; k++) if (Math.random() < 1 / 3) discounted++;
428	      need -= discounted;
429	      if (need <= 0) continue;
430	    }
431	    // 组材料（groupOf:占位 id/哨兵 id 统一判定,ConsumeFromInventory :451 语义——

437	        const iid = invIndexOfVanilla(g);
438	        if (iid < 0) continue;
439	        const take = Math.min(inv.countOf(iid), need);
440	        if (take > 0) { inv.remove(iid, take); need -= take; }
441	      }
442	      // 阶段一已过,残余只可能来自折扣外并发,放行不吞
443	    } else {
444	      // 单 id 材料：扣后同步递减 need（ConsumeItemsFrom :252-275 语义）——旧代码
445	      // 扣背包不减 need，接附近箱扣料后会重复扣箱（2026-08-13 随箱联动修复）
446	      const iid = invIndexOfVanilla(req.id);
447	      if (iid >= 0) {
448	        const take = Math.min(inv.countOf(iid), need);
449	        inv.remove(iid, take);
450	        need -= take;
451	      }
452	    }
453	    // ---- 箱/bank 扣减（CraftingRequests.Consume :220-235：先背包后箱,
454	    // 背包扣净后 need≤0 本段自然空转;ConsumeItemsFrom :242-285：槽序扫,
455	    // stack>need 部分扣、否则整格清空,need 归零即停。组槽按 Matches 任一成员）----
456	    if (sources && need > 0) {
457	      for (const src of sources) {
458	        if (need <= 0) break;
459	        const items = src.items;
460	        for (let i = 0; i < items.length && need > 0; i++) {
461	          const it = items[i];
462	          if (!it || it.stack <= 0 || !reqMatchesItem(r, req.id, it.id)) continue;
463	          if (it.stack > need) { it.stack -= need; need = 0; }
464	          else { need -= it.stack; items[i] = null; }
465	        }
466	      }
467	    }
468	  }
469	  return true;
470	}

472	/** 原版 item id → Inventory 内部 item id（缓存） */
473	const _iidCache = new Map<number, number>();
474	export function invIndexOfVanilla(vid: number): number {
475	  let hit = _iidCache.get(vid);
476	  if (hit !== undefined) return hit;
477	  const key = VID_TO_KEY.get(vid);
478	  // ITEM_DEFS 下标即内部 id——线性扫一次后缓存
479	  for (let i = 0; i < ITEM_DEFS.length; i++) {
480	    const d = ITEM_DEFS[i];
481	    const v = d.vid ?? (d.key.startsWith('vi_') ? parseInt(d.key.slice(3), 10) : -1);
482	    if (v === vid) { hit = i; break; }
483	  }
484	  hit ??= -1;
485	  _iidCache.set(vid, hit);
486	  return hit;
487	}

 "old_string": "// ---- 输出端去重（同 create+tile+材料序列 视为同一条——原版源码本身有重复注册，如\n//      4022 Seafood Dinner@烹饪锅 重复 14 次；运行时 list 重复渲染无意义）----\n{\n  const seen = new Set();\n  const unique = [];\n  for (const r of out) {\n    const key = `${r.create}|${r.tile}|${r.items.map((i) => `${i.id}x${i.stack}`).sort().join(',')}`;\n    if (seen.has(key)) continue;\n    seen.add(key);\n    unique.push(r);\n  }\n  out.length = 0;\n  out.push(...unique);\n}\n\nconst doc = {\n  count: out.length,\n  groups: GROUP_ID,\n  groupItems: GROUP_ITEMS,\n  tileCountsAs: TILE_COUNTS_AS,\n  recipes: out,\n};\nwriteFileSync(`${ROOT}/game/src/data/vanilla-recipes.json`, JSON.stringify(doc));\nconsole.error(`配方 ${out.length} 条 / 组 ${Object.keys(GROUP_ID).length} / 站点继承 ${Object.keys(TILE_COUNTS_AS).length}`);\nconsole.error('样例:', JSON.stringify(out[0]), JSON.stringify(out[1] ?? null));",
 "new_string": "// ---- 输出端去重（同 create+tile+材料序列 视为同一条——原版源码本身有重复注册，如\n//      4022 Seafood Dinner@烹饪锅 重复 14 次；运行时 list 重复渲染无意义）----\nfunction dedupKey(r) {\n  return `${r.create}|${r.tile}|${r.items.map((i) => `${i.id}x${i.stack}`).sort().join(',')}`;\n}\n{\n  const seen = new Set();\n  const unique = [];\n  for (const r of out) {\n    const key = dedupKey(r);\n    if (seen.has(key)) continue;\n    seen.add(key);\n    unique.push(r);\n  }\n  out.length = 0;\n  out.push(...unique);\n}\n\nconst JSON_PATH = `${ROOT}/game/src/data/vanilla-recipes.json`;\n\nif (process.argv.includes('--append-loops')) {\n  // ---- 追加模式（2026-08-13 循环段补漏落表用）：只把差集条目追加进现存 json，\n  //      既有条目原样保留（配方数据高频在途，禁止整文件重生成覆盖他人增量）。\n  //      同时剔除一条历史循环残影：create 5547 @114 仅 [3306x1]——旧提取器漏掉\n  //      `SetDefaults(l)` 的配重材料后折出的半截配方，原版无此配方。 ----\n  const existing = JSON.parse(readFileSync(JSON_PATH, 'utf8'));\n  const have = new Set(existing.recipes.map(dedupKey));\n  const ARTIFACT = dedupKey({ create: 5547, tile: 114, items: [{ id: 3306, stack: 1 }] });\n  const before = existing.recipes.length;\n  existing.recipes = existing.recipes.filter((r) => dedupKey(r) !== ARTIFACT);\n  const removed = before - existing.recipes.length;\n  let added = 0;\n  for (const r of out) {\n    if (have.has(dedupKey(r))) continue;\n    existing.recipes.push(r);\n    have.add(dedupKey(r));\n    added++;\n  }\n  existing.count = existing.recipes.length;\n  writeFileSync(JSON_PATH, JSON.stringify(existing));\n  console.error(`追加 ${added} 条 / 剔除循环残影 ${removed} 条 / 终态 ${existing.count} 条`);\n  process.exit(0);\n}\n\nconst doc = {\n  count: out.length,\n  groups: GROUP_ID,\n  groupItems: GROUP_ITEMS,\n  tileCountsAs: TILE_COUNTS_AS,\n  recipes: out,\n};\nwriteFileSync(JSON_PATH, JSON.stringify(doc));\nconsole.error(`配方 ${out.length} 条 / 组 ${Object.keys(GROUP_ID).length} / 站点继承 ${Object.keys(TILE_COUNTS_AS).length}`);\nconsole.error('样例:', JSON.stringify(out[0]), JSON.stringify(out[1] ?? null));"
}
```

77:export const ITEM_DEFS: ItemDef[] = [];
export const ITEM_DEFS: ItemDef[] = [];
const byKey: Record<string, number> = {};
export const ITEM_BY_KEY: Record<string, number> = byKey;

/** 1.4.5.6 堆叠上限（Item.cs:66 CommonMaxStack=9999 默认 + SetDefaults 全链仅 11 处覆盖；
 *  1405 有 1844 处逐物品赋值,1.4.4 改制统一为 9999）。vi_ 物品一律按此表,
 *  手写 maxStack 对 vi_ 键不生效——反编译源为准 */
const VANILLA_MAX_STACK: Record<number, number> = {
  71: 100, 72: 100, 73: 100,            // 铜银金币（白金 74 无覆盖=9999,DoCoins 只对 71-73 :38570）
  58: 1, 184: 1,                        // 早期特殊件
  1734: 1, 1735: 1, 1867: 1, 1868: 1, 1922: 1, 3388: 1,  // 任务/钥匙族
};
const vanillaMaxStackOf = (key: string): number | undefined => {
  const m = /^vi_(\d+)_/.exec(key);
  if (!m) return undefined;                 // 非 vi_:legacy 手写值管辖
  return VANILLA_MAX_STACK[Number(m[1])] ?? 9999;
};

function item(d: Partial<ItemDef> & { key: string; name: string }) {
  byKey[d.key] = ITEM_DEFS.length;
  // vi_ 物品:原版堆叠表权威（覆盖表 ?? 9999,手写 maxStack 一律忽略——1.4.4 改制
  // 后逐物品值已废弃,仅 11 处例外）;legacy/place_ 物品维持手写值(缺省 999)
  const base = vanillaMaxStackOf(d.key) ?? (d.maxStack ?? 999);
  ITEM_DEFS.push({ ...d, maxStack: base } as ItemDef);
}

```

...FAKE_CHEST_PAIRS.map(([c]) => c),\n      // 5547 六条（create 重复，单独点数）\n    ]);\n    let missing = 0;\n    for (const c of expected) {\n      if (!VANILLA_RECIPES.some((r) => r.create === c)) expect(VANILLA_RECIPES.some((r) => r.create === 4880)).toBe(false);\n  });\n});\n\ndescribe('循环配方 craftOne 端到端', () => {\n  /** 装材料 → 断言环境门/可合成次数 → consumeMaterials → 校验产物 key 与扣料 */\n  function craftOne(r: VanillaRecipe, mats: Array<[number, number]>, sheets: number[]) {\n    const inv = new Inventory();\n    mats.forEach(([vid, stack], k) => {\n      const iid = invIndexOfVanilla(vid);\n      expect(iid, `材料 ${vid} 未注册`).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(0);\n      inv.slots[10 + k] = { id: iid, stack };\n    });\n    // 站点门：无站点不可用，有站点可用\n    expect(envOk(r, ctx([]))).toBe(r.tile < 0);\n    expect(envOk(r, ctx(sheets))).toBe(true);\n    const owned = collectOwnedItems(inv);\n    expect(craftableCount(r, owned)).toBe(1);\n    expect(consumeMaterials(inv, r, 1, false)).toBe(true);\n    for (const [vid, stack] of mats) {\n      expect(inv.countOf(invIndexOfVanilla(vid))).toBe(0 + (inv.slots.some((s) => s && s.id === invIndexOfVanilla(vid)) ? 0 : 0));\n      void stack;\n    }\n 组聚合，RequiredItemEntry.Matches :35-42）', () => {\n    const r = VANILLA_RECIPES.find((x) => x.create === 2114 && x.tile === 106)!;\n    const inv = new Inventory();\n    // 北方木 5215 代替木 9（Wood 组成员）\n   /** 每 tick 维护（Player.fixedUpdate 挂点）：能量恢复 + 地面飞行时量回充 + 帧态机 */
  upkeep(p: MountedPlayerView): void {
    if (!this.active || this.isCart) return;
    this.abilityRecovery();
    if (p.onGround || p.vy === 0) {
      this.resetFlightTime(p);
      this.fatigueRecovery();
    }
    this.tryStartHover(p);
    this.updateFrameDispatch(p);
    this.updateBodyRotation(p);
  }

  // ---- 渲染信息 ----

  /** 玩家绘制纵向偏移（Mount.PlayerOffset :409-423：playerYOffsets[当前帧]） */
  get playerOffset(): number {
    const d = this.data;
    if (!d || !d.playerYOffsets?.length) return 0;
    if (this.frame >= d.totalFrames) return 0;
    return d.playerYOffsets[this.frame] ?? 0;
  }

  get textures(): { back?: string; front?: string; backExtra?: string; frontExtra?: string } {
    const d = this.data;
    if (!d) return {};
    const f = (n?: string) => (n ? `vanilla/${n}` : undefined);
    return { back: f(d.backTexture), front: f(d.frontTexture), backExtra: f(d.backTextureExtra), frontExtra: f(d.frontTextureExtra) };
  }
}

/** 物品 id（vanilla）→ 坐骑 id；非召唤物返回 -1 */

  return m === undefined ? -1 : m;

}

28	  const nums = m[1].split(',').map((x) => parseInt(x.trim(), 10));
29	  const table = { __default: nums[0] };

34	/** 算式求值（只认 数字 / 加减乘除 / 括号 / 已绑定循环变量；整数除法取整） */
35	function evalExpr(expr, vars) {
36	  const tokens = expr.match(/\d+|[A-Za-z_]\w*|[()+\-*/]/g);
37	  if (!tokens) throw new Error(`算式无法 tokenize: ${expr}`);
38	  const prec = { '+': 1, '-': 1, '*': 2, '/': 2 };
39	  const outQ = [], opSt = [];
40	  let prev = '';
41	  for (const tk of tokens) {
42	    if (/^\d+$/.test(tk)) outQ.push(+tk);
43	    else if (/^[A-Za-z_]/.test(tk)) {
44	      if (!(tk in vars)) throw new Error(`算式未绑定变量 ${tk}: ${expr}`);
45	      outQ.push(vars[tk]);
46	    } else if (tk === '(') opSt.push(tk);
47	    else if (tk === ')') {
48	      while (opSt.length && opSt[opSt.length - 1] !== '(') outQ.push(opSt.pop());
49	      if (!opSt.length) throw new Error(`算式括号失配: ${expr}`);
50	      opSt.pop();
51	    } else {
52	      // 一元负号（前一个 token 是运算符/开括号/空）
53	      if (tk === '-' && (prev === '' || prev === '(' || prec[prev])) outQ.push(0);
54	      while (opSt.length && prec[opSt[opSt.length - 1]] >= prec[tk]) outQ.push(opSt.pop());
55	      opSt.push(tk);
56	    }
57	    prev = tk;
58	  }
59	  while (opSt.length) {
60	    const op = opSt.pop();
61	    if (op === '(') throw new Error(`算式括号失配: ${expr}`);
62	    outQ.push(op);
63	  }
64	  const st = [];
65	  for (const tk of outQ) {
66	    if (typeof tk === 'number') { st.push(tk); continue; }
67	    const b = st.pop(), a = st.pop();
68	    if (a === undefined || b === undefined) throw new Error(`算式操作数不足: ${expr}`);
69	    st.push(tk === '+' ? a + b : tk === '-' ? a - b : tk === '*' ? a * b : Math.trunc(a / b));
70	  }
71	  if (st.length !== 1) throw new Error(`算式求值异常: ${expr}`);
72	  return st[0];
73	}

83	    return String(hit);
84	  });
85	  out = out.replace(/\b(\w+)\[([^\]]+)\]/g, (whole, name, idx) => {
86	    const arr = env.arrays[name];
87	    if (!arr) return whole;
88	    const p = idx.split(',').map((x) => evalExpr(x, { [v]: val }));
89	    const hit = arr[p[0]]?.[p[1]];
90	    if (hit === undefined) throw new Error(`${name}[${p}] 缺映射`);
91	    return String(hit);
92	  });
93	  // 余下的变量算式只出现在 SetDefaults 参数位
94	  out = out.replace(/SetDefaults\(([^()]*(?:\([^()]*\)[^()]*)*)\)/g, (whole, e) => {
95	    if (!/\b[A-Za-z_]\w*\b/.test(e.replace(/ItemID|Sets/g, ''))) return whole;
96	    return `SetDefaults(${evalExpr(e, { [v]: val })})`;
97	  });
98	  return out;
99	}

101	/** for 循环 + int[,] 数组声明预处理：循环体逐值内联展开；数组声明收录进 env 并摘除 */
102	function flattenLoops(lines, env) {
103	  const out = [];
104	  let i = 0;
105	  while (i < lines.length) {
106	    const l = lines[i].trim();
107	    // int[,] 二维数组声明（可跨行，`{ a, b },` 逐行）——只收 int[,]，不碰模板的 int[] obj
108	    if (/^int\[\,]\s*(\w+)\s*=\s*new int\[\d+, \d+\]$/.test(l)) {
109	      const name = l.match(/^int\[\,]\s*(\w+)/)[1];
110	      const cells = [];
111	      let j = i;
112	      for (; j < lines.length; j++) {
113	        for (const m of lines[j].matchAll(/\{\s*(-?\d+)\s*,\s*(-?\d+)\s*\}/g)) {
114	          cells.push([+m[1], +m[2]]);
115	        }
116	        if (lines[j].includes('};')) break;
117	      }
118	      env.arrays[name] = cells;
119	      i = j + 1;
120	      continue;
121	    }
122	    // 上界两种形态：字面量（n <= 8）/ 一维长度（j < array.GetLength(0)，env.arrays 收录过）
123	    const m = l.match(/^for \(int (\w+) = (-?\d+); \1 (<=|<|>=|>) (-?\d+|(\w+)\.GetLength\(0\)); \1\+\+\)$/);
124	    if (!m) { out.push(lines[i]); i++; continue; }
125	    const [, v, loS, op, hiS, arrName] = m;
126	    const hi = arrName ? (env.arrays[arrName]?.length ?? -1) : +hiS;
127	    // 收集循环体（`{` 起始行到括号配平行）
128	    let j = i, depth = 0, started = false;
129	    for (; j < lines.length; j++) {
130	      for (const ch of lines[j]) {
131	        if (ch === '{') { depth++; started = true; } else if (ch === '}') depth--;
132	      }
133	      if (started && depth === 0) break;
134	    }
135	    const body = flattenLoops(lines.slice(i + 1, j), env);
136	    const lo = +loS;
137	    const step = op === '<=' || op === '<' ? 1 : -1;
138	    const n = op === '<=' ? hi - lo + 1
139	      : op === '<' ? hi - lo
140	        : op === '>=' ? lo - hi + 1 : lo - hi;
141	    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
142	      const val = lo + step * k;
143	      for (const bl of body) out.push(substLine(bl, v, val, env));
144	    }
145	    i = j + 1;
146	  }

```

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-13T12:45:17.976Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
The code looks correct. One subtle issue in `substLine`'s third replace: the SetDefaults argument regex `[^()]*(?:\([^()]*\)[^()]*)*` — this permits one level of nested parens. For `SetDefaults(ItemID.Sets.TextureCopyLoad[i])` — but that was already replaced in step 1, so by step 3 it's `SetDefaults(48)`. If step 1 somehow left it (different set name), step 3's `e` would be `ItemID.Sets.TextureCopyLoad[i]` and the identifier guard would try evalExpr on it → throws "算式未绑定变量 ItemID" — acceptable loud failure rather than silent wrong data.

One more consideration: the `evalExpr` identifier check `if (!(tk in vars))` — for `{ [v]: val }` objects, `in` works. Good.

Also there's an edge: `substLine` step 2 array replacement `\b(\w+)\[([^\]]+)\]` — could match `requiredItem[0]` if someone declared an array named requiredItem — impossible in env. Fine.

Now — one robustness concern for FUTURE regenerations: if someone adds a new loop form the parser doesn't cover, entries silently vanish again (the original failure mode was silent). Add a guard: flattenLoops could warn (console.error) when a `for` header line exists but doesn't match the supported forms AND its body contains currentRecipe/AddRecipe. That turns the silent failure into a loud one — valuable for future-proofing. Let me add it.

Implementation: in flattenLoops, before pushing an unmatched line, check if the trimmed line starts with 'for (' — if so, scan ahead for the loop body extent and check if it contains 'currentRecipe' or 'AddRecipe'; if yes, console.error a warning (but still push through, preserving current behavior — no, better to keep pushing so nothing changes, but warn loudly).

Hmm — but flattenLoops is called recursively for bodies; a `for` line inside a body that we can't parse would be recursed... no, recursion only happens for matched loops. An unmatched for line is pushed through and then parseLinear's line loop sees it — body lines get folded (partially) as before. Warning is the right minimal safeguard.

Let me add that guard.
```

</details>
